问题：U1700 的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络？
参考答案：U1700 是 LP87702-Q1；VIN_B0_1、VIN_B1_1、VANA、VIN_B0_2、VIN_B1_2 接 VCC3V3，FB_B0 接 VCC1V1，FB_B1 接 VCC1V8，VOUT_BST 接 VCC50，EN1 接 MCU_5V_EN。
实际回答：根据现有证据，无法完整回答“U1700的每个关键引脚分别连接到哪个网络”的问题。以下按子问题组织，说明覆盖情况、缺失信息及证据冲突。 --- ### 子问题1：U1700的关键引脚有哪些？ **状态：部分覆盖（partial）** **支持证据：** - 电路图实例 `circuit:18:instance:U1700`（来源：`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`，证据9索引）表明U1700是原理图中的一个器件，但未提供其引脚名称或编号列表。 - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`（证据18）描述了LP87702 PMIC方案，但未明确U1700就是LP87702，也未列出U1700的引脚。 **缺失信息：** - 缺少U1700的完整引脚列表（名称、编号、类型）。无法从现有证据中确认U1700的具体引脚有哪些。 - 缺少U1700的器件型号（如LP87702或TPS6287x）的确认，因此无法引用其数据手册中的引脚定义。 **缺口说明：** - 检索账本中 `sq_1` 状态为 `partial`，缺少 `spreadsheet_table` 和 `document_text` 类型的证据。 --- ### 子问题2：每个关键引脚在原理图中连接到哪个网络？ **状态：覆盖（covered）但实际证据不足** **支持证据（声称覆盖）：** - 文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中的多个片段（如 `2d268c72a4fc40c1`、`72947b599dcff97a`、`5e69a562758e3536` 等）被标记为与子问题2相关。但这些文档内容为： - 描述MCU引脚配置（如P10.6、P14.4、ESR1等），不涉及U1700。 - 描述LP87702方案的整体功能（如用MCU的P15.3使能5V输出、I2C通信设置电压等），但未给出U1700的每个引脚到具体网络的连接。 - 电路图网络证据（如 `CAN0_INH`、`CAN1_INH` 等）均连接到其他器件（U1200、U1203等），未涉及U1700。 **实际缺失信息：** - 没有任何一条证据显示U1700的具体引脚（如EN、PG、VIN、SW等）连接到哪个网络名称。 - 缺少U1700的引脚级原理图连接信息（如哪个引脚接VCC3V3、哪个引脚接GND、哪个引脚输出VCC1V8等）。 **缺口说明：** - 检索账本中 `sq_2` 状态为 `covered`，但实际支持证据不包含U1700的引脚网络连接，属于“弱证据”或误关联。 - 缺少 `circuit_design` 类型的证据（U1700的引脚网络信息）。 --- ### 证据冲突说明 根据 `coverage_matrix.conflicts`，存在以下冲突，但这些冲突涉及的是其他字段（如`ob`、`unnamed`、`none`、`gpioxx gpioyy`），并非直接针对U1700的关键引脚或网络连接。为完整起见，列出如下： | 冲突字段 | 冲突值 | 来源 | |----------|--------|------| | `ob` | `en` 和 `pg` | `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` | | `unnamed` | `0` ~ `6` 多个值 | `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` | | `none` | `1`, `a18`, `a4`, `b`, `b15`, `config`, `eq6l`, `eq6l_gpio_a0_p23.0`, 等大量值 | 同上 | | `gpioxx gpioyy` | `00`, `01`, `10`, `11` | 同上 | 这些冲突与U1700无直接关联，但表明同一文档内存在多个不一致的取值，可能影响对器件引脚功能的解读。 --- ### 总结 - **U1700的关键引脚列表**：无法从现有证据中确定。 - **每个关键引脚连接的网络**：无任何证据提供具体连接关系。 - **建议**：需要补充U1700的原理图局部截图或网络表，以及U1700的器件型号及其数据手册，才能进行引脚与网络的对应分析。

For each Functional Safety-Capable current rating, there are full-featured device variants Documentation available to aid functional safety with I²C interface and limited-featured device variants system design without I2C interface. All devices offer high efficiency Documentation available to aid functional safety and ease of use. Low-resistance power switches system design allow up to 15-A continuous output current at high Documentation available to aid functional safety ambient temperatures. system design 2.7-V to 6-V input voltage range The devices can operate in stacked mode to deliver Family of pin-to-pin compatible devices: 6-A, 9-A, higher output currents or to spread the power 12-A, and 15-A dissipation across multiple devices. Four output voltage ranges: The TPS6287x-Q1 family implements an enhanced 0.4-V to 0.71875-V in 1.25-mV steps DCS control scheme that supports fast transients with 0.4-V to 1.0375-V in 2.5-mV steps fixed-frequency operation. Devices can operatein 0.4-V to 1.675-V in 5-mV steps power save mode for maximum efficiency, or forced- 0.8-V to 3.35-V in 10-mV steps PWM mode for the best transient performance and Output voltage accuracy ±1% lowest output voltage ripple. 7-mQ and 4.5-mQ internal power MOSFETs Adjustable external compensation An optional remote sensing feature maximizes voltage Resistor-selectable start-up output voltage and the device regulation at the point-of-load, Resistor-selectable switching frequency achieves better than ±1% DC voltage accuracy under Power-save or forced-PwM operation | 9 Detailed Description 9.1overview The TPS6287x-Q1 devices are synchronous step-down (buck) DC/DC converters. These devices use an enhanced DCS control topology to achieve fast transient response while switching with a fixed frequency. Together, with their low output voltage ripple, high DC accuracy, and differential remote sensing, these devices are ideal for supplying the cores of modern high-performance processors. The family of devices includes 6-A, 9-A, 12-A, and 15-A devices. To further increase the output current capability, the user can combine multiple devices in a “stack". For example, a stack of two TPS62873-Q1 devices have a current capability of 30 A. Each device of a stack must have the same current rating to avoid that one device enters current limit too early. For each current rating, there are full-featured devices with an I2C interface and limited-featured devices without an I2C interface (see the Device Options). The user can use a device variant without I2C in exactly the same way as a device variant with I?C, except that: · The user must connect the unused SCL and SDA pins to GND · The user must be aware of the (fixed) factory settings for parameters and functions that are programmable in the I²C device variants. 9.2 Functional Block Diagram 9.3FeatureDescription 9.3.1 Fixed-Frequency DCS Control Topology TPS6287x-Q1 devices. This topology is comprised of an inner emulated current loop, a middle direct feedback loop, and an outer voltage-regulating loop. 9.3.2 Forced PwM and Power Save Modes The device can control the inductor current in three different ways to regulate the output: Pulse-width modulation with continuous inductor current (PWM-CCM) Pulse-width modulation with discontinuous inductor current (PWM-DCM) Pulse-frequency modulation with discontinuous inductor current and pulse skipping (PFM-DCM) During PWM-CCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is continuous (see Figure 9-2). PwM operation achieves the lowest output voltage ripple and the best transient performance. During PWM-DCM operation, the device switches at a constant frequency and the inductor current is discontinuous (see Figure 9-3). In this mode, the device controls the peak inductor current to maintain the selected switching frequency while still being able to regulate the output. | Unnamed: 0：nan; Unnamed: 1：nan; Unnamed: 2：nan; 问题：网络 VCC3V3 上连接了哪些主要芯片？
参考答案：VCC3V3 有 52 个连接；主要 IC 至少包括 U1700、U1800、U1500、U1802、U1804 和 U1600。
实际回答：基于提供的证据，以下是对用户问题的回答。 ### 网络 VCC3V3 上连接的主要芯片 根据检索到的原理图（circuit_design）和设计文档（document_text），网络 VCC3V3 及其衍生网络连接了多个主要芯片。这些芯片按功能可分为电源管理芯片、主处理器、存储器、接口芯片等。 #### 1. 直接连接在 VCC3V3 网络上的主要芯片 根据原理图[1]中 `Net VCC3V3` 的连接信息，以下主要芯片直接连接到该电源网络： * **电源管理芯片：** * `U1500`：其 `VIN` 引脚连接 VCC3V3。根据设计文档[5]，U1500 是一个用于产生 VCC3V3_ETH 的负载开关（TPS22918）。 * `U1600`：其 `VCCIN` 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * `U1700`：其 `VIN_B0_1`, `VIN_B1_1`, `VANA`, `VIN_B0_2`, `VIN_B1_2` 等多个引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * `U1800`：其 `VIN` 和 `MODE/SYNC` 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * `U1802`：其 `VIN` 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * `U1804`：其 `VIN` 引脚连接 VCC3V3。 * **主控制器：** * `U900C`：其 `VEXT` 和 `VFLEX` 引脚分别连接 VCC3V3_MCU 网络，该网络由 VCC3V3 供电[6]。 * **连接器：** * `X2000`：其引脚 1 和 3 连接 VCC3V3。 #### 2. 连接在 VCC3V3 衍生网络上的主要芯片 VCC3V3 通过电源转换芯片或负载开关，为多个下游网络供电，这些网络连接到不同的主要芯片。 * **VCC3V3_MCU 网络[6]**: 该网络由 VCC3V3 经过过压保护电路后产生[19]，为 MCU 及其相关电路供电。连接的主要芯片包括： * `U1200`, `U1203`, `U1204`, `U1205`: 这些是 DDR 存储器芯片的 `VI/O` 引脚[6]。 * `U1302`, `U1303`, `U1304`, `U1305`: 这些是 DDR 存储器芯片的 `VI/O` 引脚[6]。 * `U503`: 其 `VCCB` 引脚连接 VCC3V3_MCU[6]。 * `U900C`: 其 `VEVRSB`, `VEXT`, `VFLEX` 引脚连接 VCC3V3_MCU[6]。 * **VCC3V3_EQ 网络[4]**: 该网络由 U1802 (TPS22918) 负载开关从 VCC3V3 转换而来，主要用于 EQ6 处理器供电[14]。 * `U400-2`: 其 `VDD_RING_8_0` 和 `VDD_RING_9_0` 引脚连接 VCC3V3_EQ[4]。 * `U1802`: 作为负载开关，其 `VOUT` 和 `QOD` 引脚连接此网络[4]。 * **VCC3V3_ETH 网络[5]**: 该网络由 U1500 (TPS22918) 负载开关从 VCC3V3 转换而来，用于以太网供电[15]。 * `U1501`: 其 `IN` 和 `EN` 引脚连接 VCC3V3_ETH[5]。 * **VCC3V3_CAM_FB 网络[3]**: 该网络用于摄像头反馈。 * `X1903`: 其引脚 4 连接此网络[3]。 * **VCC3V3_SW 网络[7]**: 该网络是 VCC3V3 经过过压保护电路后的输出。 * `U901`: 其 `S2` 引脚连接 VCC3V3_SW[7]。 #### 3. 主要芯片功能总结 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中，同一字段被赋予了 `b` 和 `qm` 两个不同的值。 2. **`none` 字段冲突**：在文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中，存在大量条目（如 `1`, `b`, `d20`, `e20`, `hi-z`, `mcu_3v3_eq_en` 等）被放在同一个 `none` 字段中，这些条目之间没有明确的区分，表明其内容或格式可能存在不一致。 3. **`ecu connector` 字段冲突**：在文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中，同一字段被指定为 `x2000-54` 和 `x2000-57` 两个不同的值，指向不同的ECU连接器引脚。 4. **`ob` 字段冲突**：在文档 `FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf` 中，同一字段被赋予了 `en` 和 `pg` 两个不同的值。 ### 缺失信息 * 尽管列出了连接在 VCC3V3 网络上的大量芯片（如U1600, U1700, U1800, U1804等），但现有证据**未明确说明这些芯片的具体型号或功能**。例如，U1600、U1700等芯片在原理图中被连接，但在设计文档中未找到对应描述。 * 对于 `VCC3V3_MCU` 网络上连接的 `U1200` 等芯片，证据仅指出它们是 DDR 存储器，但**未明确其具体型号**。 * **缺少最终确定的、无冲突的 `hw interface component` 值**，无法确定该组件的具体接口类型。 * **缺少最终确定的、无冲突的 `ecu connector` 值**，无法确定该功能的连接器引脚分配。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ(QM(B)) VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。 VCC1V8 to VCC1V8_EQ for EQ power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. </td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F07/F23/F25</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>WKUP模式、运行模式、OFF DELAY、下电模式 WKUP mode, run mode, OFF DELAY, power down mode</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>下电模式 Power off 当外部唤醒信号无效时，一级电源使能信号无效不工作，系统不工作。 Power off When the external wake-up signal is invalid, the primary power enable signal is invalid and does not work, and the system does not work WKUP模式 当唤醒信号有效后，系统一级电源被使能，开始输出VCC3V3电源。 WKUP mode When the wake-up signal is valid, the system-level power supply is enabled and the output of vCC3V3 power supply is started OFF DELAY 唤醒信号无效后，MCU将MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0信号持续配置为逻辑高，开始OFF DELAY功能。在完成数据保护后， MCU开始将MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0信号配置为逻辑低，系统进入下电模式。 OFF DELAY After the wake-up signal is invalid, the MCU continues to configure the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 signal to logic high and starts the OFF DELAY function. After data protection is completed, the MCU starts to configure the MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 signal to logic low, and the system enters the power down mode. 运行模式 MCU工作后使用ADC采样采集WKUP_ACQ信号端电压，同时将MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0信号配置为逻辑高辅助使能一级电源。 Run mode After MCU works, WKUP_ACQ signal terminal voltage is collected by ADC sampling, and MCU_WKUP_HOLD_P12.0 signal is configured as logic high auxiliary enable level 1 power supply. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td> </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirements specification. When VCC3V3 <4.03V, the cathode pin is in the high resistance state. When VCC3V3 <VGs threshold (Q1701), NMOS is off, PMOS is off, and VCC3V3_SW has no electricity. 2.When 4.03V>VCC3V3>VGS threshold (Q1701), NMOS opens, PMOS opens, and VCC3V3_sw and VCC3V3 pass through. 3.When VCC3V3>4.03V, the cathode pin output logic is low (up to 1V), NMOS is off, PMOS is off, and VCC3V3_Sw has no power, which plays an overvoltage protection role on the load of the later 问题：ADAS 项目的软硬件接口文档中，硬件架构包含哪些模块电路？
参考答案：HSI 2.3 节列出 KL30 电源管理、唤醒、电源、TC377 最小系统、EQ6 最小系统、摄像头最小系统、看门狗、温度采样、系统内电源电压采样、ETH、CAN/CAN FD 和 3V3 过压保护等电路。
实际回答：根据ADAS项目软硬件接口文档（来源：600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx），硬件架构包含以下模块电路： 1. KL30电源管理电路（KL30 supply management circuit） 2. 唤醒电路（Wake Up circuit） 3. 电源电路（power circuit） 4. TC377最小系统（TC377 minimum system） 5. EQ6最小系统（EQ6 minimum system） 6. 摄像头最小系统（camera minimum system） 7. 看门狗电路（Watchdog circuit） 8. 温度采样电路（Temp Sample circuit） 9. 系统内电源电压采样电源（The power System Voltage Sampling circuit） 10. ETH电路（ETH circuit） 11. CAN/CAN FD电路（CAN/CAN FD circuit） 12. 3V3过压保护电路（3V3 overvoltage protection circuit） **缺失信息：** 无。所有预期的模块电路均已在文档中明确列出。 **证据冲突：** 在文档中，关于TC377最小系统模块的电源需求，存在不同数值的表述。一处描述为“VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）”，另一处描述为“VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）”。这是两个不同电压轨的规格，并非对同一字段的冲突，但需注意其精度要求差异。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system block功能安全分解相关模块模块电路 Module circuitKL30电源管理电路 KL30 supply management circuit | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01/F06/F11</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS正常工作电压范围9~16VDC，系统需求需要对产品供电KL30进行电源管理，硬件电路对UBD电源网络进行ADC采样。 ADAS normal operating voltage range is 9~16VDC. System requirements require power management for the product power supply KL30. The hardware circuit performs ADC sampling on the UBD power supply.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>MCU_UB_SW_P00.10信号是由MCU I/O控制的数字信号，用于使能KL30电压采样功能。 MCU_UB_SW_P00.10 signal is a digital signal controlled by MCU I/O, used to enable KL30 voltage sampling function. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. MCU的ADC采集到UBD后进行电源管理，当软件诊断到UBD电压范围异常时，MCU会关断EQ和其他负载工作电源，确保ADAS功能安全。 The ADC of MCU collects UBD for power management. When the software detects that UBD voltage range is abnormal, the MCU will turn off EQ and other load power supplies to ensure the functional safety of ADAS.</td></tr></table> | requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. Power demand The MCU needs 1 channel VCC3V3_MCU (accuracy: 3.3V±10%) and 1 channel VCC1V25_MCU (accuracy: 1.2-1.35V) power supply.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F24</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>ADAS系统需求MCU外接1路100BASE-T1以太网通讯接口，用于软件调试和对外通讯。 The ADAS system requires MCU to be connected with a 100BASE-T1 Ethernet communication interface for software debugging and external communication.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>硬件设计选型汽车级芯片DP83TC812R（TI），支持100BASE-TI通讯、以太网唤醒功能，电路和PCB参考原厂规范进行设计。所用到引脚是MCU_ETH_MDIO_P21.3，MCU_ETH_MDC_P21.2，MCU_MII_TXD3_P11.0，MCU_MII_TXD2_P11.1，MCU_MII_TXD1_P11.2，MCU_MII_TXD0_P11.3，MCU_MII_TXCLK_P11.5，MCU_MII_TXDV_P11.6，MCU_MII_RXD3_P11.7，MCU_MII_RXD2_P11.8，MCU_MII_RXD1_P11.9，MCU_MII_RXD0_P11.10，MCU_MII_RXDV_P11.11，MCU_MII_RXCLK_P11.12， MCU_ETH_INTB_P20.10，PHY_RESET_N_P22.3，MCU_ETH_WAKE_P21.5。 The automotive grade chip DP83TC812R (TI) is selected for hardware design, which supports 100BASE-TI communication and Ethernet wake-up function. 问题：给板子供电的主 PMIC 是哪颗？它输出哪些电压，软件如何配置？
参考答案：主 PMIC 是 U1700，即 LP87702-Q1，输入 VCC3V3，输出包括 VCC1V1、VCC1V8 和 VCC50；软件配置依据 HSI 7.2 节 LP87702 配置说明，通过 I²C 相关信号配置。
实际回答：好的，根据提供的证据，我对您的问题进行解答。所有信息均严格基于给定的证据，对于缺失或存在冲突的信息，我已明确指出。 ### 1. 板子的主PMIC型号是什么？ **答案：** 板子的主PMIC型号是 **LP87702**。 **证据来源：** * **证据11（文档 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）**：明确提到“VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) ... 现选择 **LP87702 PMIC** 方案。” * **证据20（同一文档）**：在表格中列出“LP87702，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出”。 ### 2. 该PMIC输出哪些电压轨？ **答案：** 该LP87702 PMIC负责输出以下三个电压轨： * **VCC1V8** (1.8V) * **VCC1V1** (1.1V) * **VCC50** (5.0V) **证据来源：** * **证据11（文档 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）**：明确说明“VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。” * **证据20（同一文档）**：表格中明确列出LP87702的功能是“VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC50电压输出”。 ### 3. 如何通过软件配置该PMIC？ **答案：** 软件通过 **I2C** 通信接口对LP87702进行配置。 **具体配置方式：** 1. **通信接口**：MCU使用I2C总线（SCL和SDA引脚）与PMIC通信。相关证据显示，MCU的 `MCU_SCL_P15.4` 和 `MCU_SDA_P15.5` 引脚连接到PMIC所在的模块组。 2. **配置对象**：软件通过I2C接口 **设置PMIC内部的寄存器** 来调整其输出。 3. **配置内容**：通过I2C通信，软件可以 **设置输出电压**（特别是1.8V和1.1V）以及 **使能/禁用** 对应的输出通道。 4. **使能控制**：除了I2C配置，PMIC的5V输出（VCC50）的使能还受MCU的一个GPIO引脚控制。证据11提到，“用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出”。 **证据来源：** * **证据9（文档 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）**：明确提到“LP87702运行期间需要MCU通过I2C配置的寄存器如下所示：Table 5-2 LP87702 Configuration”。（虽然未提供具体寄存器内容，但明确了配置方法）。 * **证据11（同一文档）**：详细描述了配置方式：“用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。” * **证据6和7（电路图 825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF）**：显示了PMIC模块与MCU模块之间通过 `MCU_SCL_P15.4` 和 `MCU_SDA_P15.5` 网络连接，证实了I2C通信链路的硬件连接。 ### 缺失信息与证据冲突说明 * **缺失信息**：证据明确指出了通过I2C配置寄存器，但未提供具体的寄存器名称、地址值或配置步骤的详细描述。这些信息通常需要查阅LP87702的数据手册来获取。 * **证据冲突**：在提供的证据中，存在一些与PMIC直接配置无关的冲突，主要是在文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 内部，关于MCU（SAK-TC377TP）的报警（Alarm）ID、名称、编号等字段存在不一致。例如： * 对于“No.”字段，存在58, 59, 60, 61, 81, 82, 83, 84, 85, 86, 87, 88等多个不同取值。 * 对于“Alarm ID”字段，同时存在“alm8”和“alm9”等不同取值。 * 这些冲突是关于MCU自身安全机制的，与“如何配置PMIC”这一核心问题不直接相关，但在检索过程中被识别出来。

KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | No.：86; Alarm ID：ALM9[0]; Alarm name：Temperature overflow; Related SMs：SM[HW]:DTS:TEMPERATURE_MONITOR; Safety related ?：Y; READY OTP The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level, and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. The OTP configuration is loaded into the register. STANDBY The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The voltage converter is disabled and the reference level, control, and bias circuits of the LP87702-Q1 are enabled. All registers can be read and written by the host processor through the system serial port. The voltage converter can perform enabling output as needed. ACTIVE The supply voltage VANA is above the VANAUVLO level and the NRST voltage is above the threshold level. The regulated DC/DC converter is in the enabled state. All the registers can be read and written by the MCU through the system serial port. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state None：HI-Z，硬件外部下拉; None：—; SW Variable Name or SW Function Name：— ——Port1 | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F20/F21/F22/F23</td></tr><tr><td>运行模式 Operation Mode</td><td>运行模式、待机模式、Sleep模式、OFF模式、Overtemp模式 Run mode, standby mode, Sleep mode, OFF mode, Overtemp mode.</td></tr><tr><td>模式定义 Definition of Operation mode</td><td>TCAN1044A： 1、 运行模式 STB设置为低电平使能Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、 待机模式 STB设置为高电平使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据。 1.Run mode The STB is set to low to enable Normal mode, and the transceiver can send and receive data through the bus CANH and CANL. 2.Standby mode The STB is set to high level enable Standby mode, and the transceiver will no longer send the received data. TCAN1145： 1、Normal模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=111进入Normal模式，收发器可以通过总线CANH和CANL发送接收数据。 2、Standby模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=100，使能Standby模式，收发器将不再发送接收数据，但INH引脚依然有效。 3、Sleep模式 通过SPI3配置寄存器MC=001或者发生欠压事件，CAN收发器进入sleep模式。该模式下INH为高阻态。 与TJA1145寄存器地址有差别，软件开发需要注意。 4、Off模式 当电源电压过低无法为IC供电，收发器进入Off模式，该模式下CAN引脚和INH引脚为高阻态。 5、Overtemp模式 当芯片整体温度高于阈值，会进入过温模式，该模式下收发器的发送端和接收端被禁用，INH引脚为高阻态。 Normal mode The Normal mode is entered through SPI3 configuration register MC=111, and the transceiver can send and receive data through buses CANH and CANL. Standby mode By configuring the SPI3 register MC=100 to enable the Standby mode, the transceiver will no longer send the received data, but the INH pin will remain active. Sleep mode Through SPI3 configuration register MC=001 or an undervoltage event occurs, the CAN transceiver goes into sleep mode. In this mode, INH is in the high impedance state. Off mode When the supply voltage is too low to power the IC, the transceiver enters the Of mode, in which the CAN pin and INH pin are in the high resistance state. Overtemp mode When the overall chip temperature is higher than the threshold, it will enter the over-temperature mode, in which the transmitter and receiver of the transceiver are disabled, and the INH pin is in the high impedance state. </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td>TCAN1044A： TCAN1145： </td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td></td></tr></table> | Register：nan; Among these voltages, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC0V75, 0V75_MIPI_DDR, VCC_1V8_CAM, and VCC1V1_CAM have voltage ranges within the measurement range and are directly sampled. VCC3V3_EQ and VCC3V3_CAM are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_EQ is sampled using AN2, VCC1V8 using AN3, VCC1V1 using AN4, VCC1V8_EQ using AN5, VCC3V3_CAM using AN12, VCC1V1_CAM using AN13, VCC_1V8_CAM using AN14, 0V75_MIPI_DDR using AN18, and VCC0V75 using AN19. CAN&ETH电源电压采样 系统需求MCU对LP87702、TPS22918的输出电压VCC3V3_ETH，VCC50进行ADC采样和异常监控，VCC3V3_ETH和VCC50经过两个10K 1%精度电阻分压后采样。VCC3V3_ETH用AN8引脚采样，VCC50用AN1引脚采样。 CAN&ETH power supply voltage sampling The system requirements specify that the MCU must perform ADC sampling and abnormal monitoring on the output voltages VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 from the LP87702 and TPS22918. Both VCC3V3_ETH and VCC50 are sampled after being divided by two 10K resistors with 1% precision. Specifically, VCC3V3_ETH is sampled using AN8, and VCC50 is sampled using AN1.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>模块序号 Module number</td><td>模块名称 Module name</td><td>功能定义 Function 问题：0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 接 VCC3V3，VOSNS 对应 VCC0V75；配置要求见 HSI 7.3 节 TPS62872 配置。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，针对您的问题“0.75V 电源由哪颗芯片产生，输入是什么，文档有什么配置要求？”，回答如下： ### 1. 产生0.75V电源的芯片是哪个？ **芯片型号：SA23002（矽力杰/Silergy）** - **来源说明**：证据[14]（文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）明确描述：“VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR应用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。” 证据[20]（同一文档）也指出：“VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed.” - **证据覆盖度**：该子问题（sq_1）在检索账本中状态为“covered”，证据充分。 ### 2. 该芯片的输入电源是什么？ **输入电源：VCC3V3 (3.3V)** - **来源说明**：证据[14]和[20]均明确指出，该电源转换路径为“VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR”或“VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR”，即输入为VCC3V3。 - **证据覆盖度**：该子问题（sq_2）在检索账本中状态为“covered”，证据充分。 ### 3. 文档中对该芯片的配置要求有哪些？ 根据文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`，配置要求如下： - **使能控制**：使用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 - **来源说明**：证据[14]描述：“用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。” 证据[20]也提到：“用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。” - **硬件连接**：该芯片的使能引脚（EN）连接至网络`MCU_VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN`，该网络连接至MCU的P22.1引脚（U900E.P22.1）和SA23002芯片（U1804）的EN引脚。 - **来源说明**：证据[1]（电路设计文件`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）显示，网络`MCU_VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR_EN`连接了`U1804.EN`（即SA23002的使能引脚）和`U900E.P22.1`（MCU引脚）。 - **证据覆盖度**：该子问题（sq_3）在检索账本中状态为“covered”，配置要求主要涉及使能控制，未提及I2C配置或输出电压设置等具体寄存器配置细节。 ### 缺失信息说明 - **输出网络名称**：文档中未明确给出0.75V电源的具体网络名称（如`VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR`），但根据上下文推断，该电源输出为`0V75_MIPI_DDR`。 - **具体配置参数**：文档未提供SA23002芯片的详细配置参数，如输出电压设定、软启动时间、开关频率等。仅指明了使能引脚控制方式。如需更详细的配置要求，需查阅SA23002的官方数据手册。 ### 证据冲突说明 在检索的证据中，存在以下冲突，但**均与SA23002芯片无关**，而是涉及另一款芯片TPS62872（用于产生VCC0V75）。这些冲突不会影响本问题的答案，但为保持透明，列出如下： - **LSB（最低有效位）**：来自文档`FF4D6750FC7339E769C3F6246C6F971C.pdf`（TPS62872数据手册）的证据[12]中，对FSEL引脚的LSB检测逻辑给出了两种不同描述（“LSB = 1”和“LSB = 0”），但未在上下文校准。 - **电压设定点（VSET）**：同一文档证据[17]描述了四种电压范围，其中“Range 4”的起始电压为0.8V，与其他范围（0.4V起始）不同。 - **开关频率（fsw）**：证据[13]和[19]给出了不同的典型开关频率值（2.25 MHz、1.5 MHz、2.5 MHz、3 MHz），这些是TPS62872在不同配置下的选项。 - **其他字段冲突**：在文档`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`中，存在大量无关字段的冲突（如各种网络名称、引脚号等），这些是系统级描述，不影响本问题的核心结论。 **总结**：0.75V电源由芯片**SA23002**产生，输入为**VCC3V3**，配置要求主要为**使用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片**。文档未提供其他配置参数，缺失信息已列出。证据冲突不涉及SA23002，已单独说明。

Then, when the user pulls the EN pin high, the device starts up with the desired start-up voltage. Note that if the user changes the device settings through I?C while the device is ramping, the device ignores the changes until the ramp is complete 9.3.6.4 Dynamic Voltage Scaling If the user changes the output voltage setpoint while the DC/DC converter is operating, the device ramps up or down to the new voltage setting in a controlled way. The VRAMP[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register sets the slew rate when the device ramps from one voltage to another during DVS (see Table 9-6). Note that ramping the output to a higher voltage requires additional output current, so that during DvS, the converter must generate a total output current given by: dVour lour = lour c) + Cour dVOouT where · lout is the total current the converter must generate while ramping to a higher voltage. louT(Dc) is the DC load current.· Cout is the total output capacitance. dVout/dt is the slew rate of the output voltage (programmable in the range 0.5 mV/μs to 10 mV/us). For correct operation, ensure that the total output current during DVS does not exceed the current limit of the device. 9.3.7 Compensation (CoMP) The CoMP pin is the connection point for an external compensation network. A series-connected resistor and capacitor to GOSNS is sufficient for typical applications. The series-connected resistor also provides enough scope to optimize the loop response for a wide range of operating conditions. When using multiple devices in a stacked configuration, all devices share a common compensation network, and the COMP pin makes sure there is equal current sharing between them (see Section 9.3.17). 9.3.8 ModeSelection and Clock Synchronization (MODE/SYNC) A high level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects forced PWM operation. A low level on the MODE/SYNC pin selects power save operation, in which, the device automatically transitions between PWM and PFM, according to the load conditions. | <table><caption> Table 9-13. resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. For compatibility with future device variants,</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >program these bits to 0.</td></tr> <tr><td >3-2</td><td >VRANGE</td><td >R/W</td><td >10b</td><td >Output voltage range</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >00b = 0.4 V to 0.71875 V in 1.25-mV steps</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >01b = 0.4 V to 1.0375 V in 2.5-mV steps</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >10b = 0.4 V to 1.675 V in 5-mV steps</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >11b = 0.8 V to 3.35 V in 10-mV steps</td></tr> <tr><td >1-0</td><td >SSTIME</td><td >R/W</td><td >01b</td><td >Soft-start ramp time </td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >00b = 0.5 ms</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >01b = 1 ms</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >10b = 2 ms</td></tr> <tr><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td >11b = 4 ms</td></tr> </table> | window comparator monitors the output voltage, and the output of the comparator goes high if the output voltage is either less than 95% (typical) or greater than 105% (typical) of the nominal output voltage. The output of the window comparator is deglitched - the typical deglitch time is 40 μs - and then used to drive the open-drain PG pin. During DVS activity, when the DC/DC converter transitions from one output voltage setting to another, the output voltage can temporarily exceed the limits of the window comparator and pull the PG pin low. The device has a feature to disable this behavior. If PGBLNKDVS = 1 in the CONTROL3 register, the device ignores the output of the power-good window comparator while DvS is active. Note that the PG pin is always low, regardless of the output of the window comparator, when: · The device is in thermal shutdown. The device is disabled. The device is in undervoltage lockout.· The device is in soft start. 9.3.14.2 Secondary Device Behavior initialization, the device presets FF1 and FF2, which pulls down the PG pin and forces the device to operate in DCM. When the device completes its soft start, it resets FF2, which turns off Q1. However, in a stacked configuration, all devices share the same PG signal, and therefore the PG pin stays low until all devices in the stack have completed their soft start. When that happens, FF1 is reset and the converters operate in CCM. 9.3.15 Remote Sense The device has two pins, VOSNS and GOSNS, to remotely sense the output voltage. Remote sensing lets the converter sense the output voltage directly at the point-of-load and increases the accuracy of the output voltage regulation. 9.3.16 Thermal Warning and Shutdown The device has a two-level overtemperature detection function. If the junction temperature rises above the thermal warning threshold of 150°C (typical), the device sets the TWARN bit in the STATUS register. The device clears the TWARN bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the junction temperature is below the TWARN threshold of 130°C (typical). If the junction temperature rises above the thermal shutdown threshold of 170°C (typical), the device: · Stops switching · Pulls down the EN pin (if SINGLE = 0 in the CONTROL3 register) · Enables the output discharge (if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register) ·Sets the TSHUT bit in the STATUS register · Pulls the PG pin low | high level, LSB = 1. 9.3.6 Output Voltage Setting 9.3.6.1 Output Voltage Range The device has four different voltage ranges. The VRANGE[1:0] bits in the CONTROL1 register control which range is active (see Table 9-3). The default output voltage range after device initialization is 0.4 V to 1.675 V in 5-mV steps. Note that every change to the VRANGE[1:0] bits must be followed by a write to the VSET register, even if the value of the VSET[7:o] bits does not change. This sequence is required for the device to start to use the new voltage range. Also note that the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range uses a 0.8-V reference and the other ranges use a 0.4-V reference. Switching to and from the 0.8-V to 3.35-V range can therefore cause increased output voltage undershoot or overshoot while the device switches its internal reference. In device variants that do not have I?C, the output voltage range is factory-set to 0.4 V to 1.675 V. 9.3.6.2OutputVoltageSetpoint Together with the selected range, the VSET[7:0] bits in the VSET register control the output voltage setpoint of the device (see Table 9-4). During initialization, the device reads the state of the VSEL pin and selects the default output voltage according to Table 9-5. Note that the VSEL pin also selects the I?C target address of the device (see Table 9-10). If the user programs new output voltage setpoint (VOUT[7:0]), output voltage range (VRANGE[1:0]), or soft-start time (SSTIME[1:o]) settings when the device has already begun its soft-start sequence, the device ignores the new values until the soft-start sequence is complete. If the user changes the value of VSET[7:0] during soft start, the device first ramps to the value that VSET[7:0] had when the soft-start sequence began. Then, when soft start is complete, ramps up or down to the new value. If the user changes VOUT[7:0], VRAMP[1:0], or SSTIME[1:0] while EN is low, the device uses the new values the next time the user enables it.

问题：3.3V 电源有没有过压保护，是如何实现的？
参考答案：HSI 2.3.12 节声明存在 3V3 过压保护电路；EDF 只能用于说明观察到的二极管或 TVS 连接拓扑，不能在没有数据手册证据时断言具体阈值或保护能力。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，3.3V电源具有过压保护功能，具体实现方式如下： ### 1. 3.3V电源是否有过压保护？ **是，存在过压保护。** * **证据来源：** 硬件设计文档 [1]、[2] 明确标识了模块 `F18`（overvoltage protect）用于MCU电源VCC3V3_MCU的外部过压保护 [1]。 * **证据来源：** 文档 [2] 详细描述了该过压保护模块的功能需求：“AOU要求MCU需要硬件外部增加过压保护电路对3V3_MCU进行电压监控。” [2] * **证据来源：** 文档 [14] 的目录部分列有“3V3过压保护电路 3V3 overvoltage protection circuit”章节，进一步确认了该电路的存在。 ### 2. 过压保护的实现原理是什么？ **过压保护功能通过一个由比较器（或类似功能的器件）和一对NMOS与PMOS组成的电路实现。** * **证据来源：** 文档 [2] 的“模块方案”部分详细描述了其工作原理： 1. **正常/欠压状态：** 当VCC3V3电压低于4.03V时，比较器的阴极引脚为高阻态。此时，若VCC3V3电压低于NMOS的栅源电压（VGS）阈值，NMOS和PMOS均关闭，VCC3V3_SW无输出。当VCC3V3电压介于VGS阈值和4.03V之间时，NMOS和PMOS均导通，VCC3V3_SW与VCC3V3直通，为后级电路正常供电。 2. **过压保护状态：** 当VCC3V3电压超过4.03V时，比较器的阴极引脚输出逻辑低电平（最高1V），导致NMOS关闭，进而使PMOS也关闭，切断VCC3V3_SW的输出，从而对后级负载起到过压保护作用。 ### 缺失信息 * **电路原理图细节：** 证据中未提供该过压保护电路的完整原理图，例如具体使用的比较器型号、MOSFET型号、以及用于设定阈值4.03V的电阻分压网络的具体参数（如电阻值）。 * **其他3.3V电源轨的保护：** 证据明确指出此过压保护电路是针对“VCC3V3_MCU”的 [1]。对于系统中其他3.3V电源轨（如VCC3V3_EQ、VCC3V3_ETH），虽然有输入端的瞬态过压保护（如TVS管）[12]，但文档未明确说明它们是否也具备与VCC3V3_MCU类似的、由专用电路实现的连续过压保护功能。 ### 证据冲突 在提供的证据中，存在以下冲突，这些冲突不影响上述关于3.3V过压保护电路的回答，但需注意： 1. **MCU电源精度要求冲突：** 文档 [5] 的“模块方案”中，关于MCU电源VCC1V25_MCU的精度要求，在同一表格的两个不同位置出现了“精度：1.2-1.35v”和“精度：3.3v±10%”的描述。这属于明显的笔误或数据不一致，但3.3V电源的精度要求与该问题不直接相关。 2. **其他字段冲突：** 证据评分部分列出了多个字段冲突，例如`no.`、`alarm id`、`sri`、`alarm name`、`vih`、`lol`、`hiccupen`等，这些冲突来源于不同的文档或表格，与3.3V电源过压保护这一核心问题无关，但表明了文档内部存在不一致性。

The negative-going threshold of the UVLO function is 2.5 V (typical). If the supply voltage decreases below this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. 9.3.12 Overvoltage Lockout (OVLO) The device has an overvoltage lockout function that disables the DC/DC converter if the supply voltage is too high for correct operation. The positive-going threshold of the OVLO function is 6.3 V (typical). If the supply voltage increases above this value, the device stops switching and, if DISCHEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register, turns on the output discharge. The device automatically starts switching again - it begins a new soft-start sequence - when the supply voltage falls below 6.2 V (typical) 9.3.13 Overcurrent Protection 9.3.13.1 Cycle-by-Cycle Current Limiting If the peak inductor current increases above the high-side current limit threshold, the device turns off the high-side switch and turns on the low-side switch to ramp down the inductor current. The device only turns on the high-side switch again if the inductor current has decreased below the low-side current limit threshold. Note that because of the propagation delay of the current limit comparator, the current limit threshold in practice can be greater than the DC value specified in the Electrical Characteristics. Define the HW component functions in each operating mode, describe the transition condition between different modes.硬件运行模式 Hardware Operation Mode系统功能矩阵描述系统不同模式下各功能模块运行状态。The system function matrix describes the operation status of each function module in different modes of the system. | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F02/F03/F04/F28</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>EYEQ6-L最小系统组成：外部30MHz晶体给EYEQ6-L提供基本时钟输入，外部20MHz有源晶振提供监控时钟，外扩OSPI接口的2Gb NAND FLASH存储程序，外扩1GB的LPDDR4运存。NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_SPI进行电平转换，用于MCU读写NAND FLASH。 EYEQ6-L minimum system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | Figure 10-1. is the output voltage.· · L is the effective inductance at the peak current level. tpd is the propagation delay of the current limit comparator (typically 50 ns). 9.3.13.2HiccupMode To enable hiccup operation, ensure that HICCUPEN = 1 in the CONTROL1 register. If hiccup operation is enabled and the high-side switch current exceeds the current limit threshold on 32 consecutive switching cycles, the device: Stops switching for 128 clock cycles, after which it automatically starts switching again (it starts a new soft-start sequence) · Sets the HICCUP bit in the STATUS register Pulls the PG pin low. The PG pin stays low until the overload condition goes away and the device can start up correctly and regulate the output voltage. Note that power-good function has a deglitch circuit, which delays the rising edge of the power-good signal by 40 μs (typical). Hiccup operation continues in a repeating sequence of 32 cycles in current limit, followed by a pause of 128 cycles, followed by a soft-start attempt for as long as the output overload condition exists. The device clears the HICCUP bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition has been removed. 9.3.13.3CurrentLimitMode To enable current limit mode, ensure that HICCUPEN = 0 in the CONTROL1 register. When current limit operation is enabled, the device limits the high-side switch current cycle-by-cycle for as long as the overload condition exists. If the device limits the high-side switch current for four or more consecutive switching cycles, the device sets ILIM = 1 in the STATUS register. The device clears the ILIM bit if the user reads the STATUS register when the overload condition no longer exits. 9.3.14 Power Good (PG) The power good (PG) pin is bidirectional and has two functions: · In a standalone configuration and in the primary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an open- drain output that indicates the status of the converter or stack. · In a secondary device of a stacked configuration, the PG pin is an input that indicates when the soft-start sequence is complete and all converters in the stack can change from DCM switching to CCM switching. 9.3.14.1 Standalone orPrimaryDeviceBehavior The primary purpose of the PG pin is to indicate if the output voltage is in regulation, but it also indicates if the device is in thermal shutdown or disabled. Table 9-7 summarizes the behavior of the PG pin in a standalone or primary device.

问题：板上用 TPS22918 做了哪些电源开关？逐个给出输入、输出和使能。
参考答案：U1500：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_ETH，使能 MCU_3V3_ETH_EN；U1802：VCC3V3 到 VCC3V3_EQ，使能 MCU_3V3_EQ_EN；U1803：VCC1V8 到 VCC1V8_EQ，使能 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，板上共有三个TPS22918实例，分别用于以下电源开关： ### 1. 实例 U1500 / 功能模块 F19 * **功能描述**：VCC1V8 转 VCC1V8_EQ，用于 EQ6 系统供电。 * **输入 (Vin)**：VCC1V8。 * **输出 (Vout)**：VCC1V8_EQ。 * **使能 (EN)**：由 MCU 的 P20.0 引脚控制，使能信号名称为 `MCU_1V8_EQ_EN`。高电平使能输出，低电平关闭输出。 * **来源**： * 设计文档明确指出模块 F19 使用 TPS22918 将 “VCC1V8” 转换为 “VCC1V8_EQ”，并由 “MCU的P20.0引脚” 使能 [4]。 * 电路图文件确认存在实例 U1500，型号为 TPS22918 [17]。 * 功能定义文档将 F19 描述为 “TPS22918 负载开关，VCC1V8电压输入，VCC1V8_EQ电压输出” [1]。 * 模式定义文档描述了 MCU 通过 `MCU_1V8_EQ_EN` 信号控制 VCC1V8_EQ 电源 [2]。 ### 2. 实例 U1802 / 功能模块 F16 * **功能描述**：VCC3V3 转 VCC3V3_EQ，用于 EQ6 和摄像头系统供电。 * **输入 (Vin)**：VCC3V3。 * **输出 (Vout)**：VCC3V3_EQ。 * **使能 (EN)**：由 MCU 的 P20.3 引脚控制，使能信号名称为 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN`。高电平使能输出，低电平关闭输出。 * **来源**： * 设计文档明确指出模块 F16 使用 TPS22918 将 “VCC3V3” 转换为 “VCC3V3_EQ”，并由 “MCU的P20.3引脚” 使能 [7]。 * 电路图文件确认存在实例 U1802，型号为 TPS22918 [18]。 * 功能定义文档将 F16 描述为 “TPS22918负载开关，VCC3V3电压输入，VCC3V3_EQ输出” [1]。 * 模式定义文档描述了 MCU 通过 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 信号控制 VCC3V3_EQ 电源 [6]。 * HW Interface 文档详细描述了此连接，其中使能信号 `MCU_3V3_EQ_EN` 来自 MCU 的 P20.3 引脚 [3]。 ### 3. 实例 U1803 / 功能模块 F17 * **功能描述**：VCC3V3 转 VCC3V3_ETH，用于 MCU ETH 系统供电。 * **输入 (Vin)**：VCC3V3。 * **输出 (Vout)**：VCC3V3_ETH。 * **使能 (EN)**：由 MCU 的 P21.0 引脚控制，使能信号名称为 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN`。高电平使能输出，低电平关闭输出。 * **来源**： * 电路图文件确认存在实例 U1803，型号为 TPS22918 [19]。 * 功能定义文档将 F17 描述为 “TPS22918负载开关，VCC3V3输入，VCC3V3_ETH输出” [1]。 * 模式定义文档描述了 MCU 通过 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN` 信号控制 VCC3V3_ETH 电源 [5]。 * HW Interface 文档详细描述了此连接，其中使能信号 `MCU_3V3_ETH_EN` 来自 MCU 的 P21.0 引脚 [3]。 **缺失信息**：无缺失信息。所有三个 TPS22918 实例的输入、输出和使能连接均已明确记录。 **证据冲突**：您提供的证据冲突列表中的内容（如 `fsw`、`1`、`b`、`f14` 等）与本次关于 TPS22918 实例连接的问题无关，因此不构成冲突。

问题：以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来？
参考答案：供电链路为 VCC3V3 → U1500（TPS22918，ON=MCU_3V3_ETH_EN）→ VCC3V3_ETH → U1501（TPS74501 LDO）→ VCC1V0_ETH。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，针对“以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来”这一问题，分析如下： ### 1. 以太网 PHY 的型号及 1.0V 电源输入要求 * **芯片型号**：电路图（`825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF`）中，以太网 PHY 模块（Group 14 · Ethernet/PHY）的实例为 `U1400`。该模块通过 `PHY_MDIN`、`PHY_MDIP`、`PHY_RESET_N_P22.3`、`PHY_WAKE_P21.5` 等网络与外部连接。**然而，提供的证据中没有任何文档或数据表明确给出 `U1400` 的具体型号（如厂商、型号名）**。 * **1.0V 电源输入要求**：**证据中未提及任何关于该以太网 PHY 芯片的电源输入要求（如所需的电压、电流），也未提到其需要 1.0V 电源轨**。电路图证据显示的是数据信号（MDIN/MDIP）、复位（RESET_N）和唤醒（WAKE）等引脚，并未涉及电源引脚。 ### 2. 系统电源树中 1.0V 电源轨的转换路径及芯片 * **证据状态**：系统电源树文档（`600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx`）详细描述了多个电源转换路径，但**所有列出的电源轨（VCC0V75、VCC1V1、VCC1V8、VCC3V3、VCC5V0等）的电压值均不是 1.0V**。 * **具体路径**：文档中描述的电源转换路径包括： * **12V → VCC3V3**：使用 `LN10046` DCDC 芯片。 * **VCC3V3 → VCC0V75**：使用 `TPS62872` 芯片，用于 EQ6 内核供电。 * **VCC3V3 → VCC0V75_MIPI_DDR**：使用 `SA23002` 芯片。 * **VCC3V3 → VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC5V0**：使用 `LP87702` PMIC。 * **VCC3V3 → VCC3V3_ETH**：使用 `TPS22918` 负载开关，用于以太网供电。 * **缺失信息**：**整个系统电源树中，未定义或描述任何 1.0V 电源轨。因此，无法回答“1.0V 电源如何一级级供出来”这一问题**。 ### 3. 证据冲突 检索结果中存在证据冲突，但 **这些冲突与当前问题（以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源）无关**。冲突内容涉及文档 `600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx` 中不同字段的取值不一致，例如： * `hw interface component` 字段同时取值 `b` 和 `qm`。 * `ecu connector` 字段同时取值 `x2000-54` 和 `x2000-57`。 ### 总结与缺失信息 * **缺失项 1**：以太网 PHY 实例 `U1400` 的具体型号。 * **缺失项 2**：该以太网 PHY 的电源输入要求（电压、电流）。 * **缺失项 3**：系统电源树中，**不存在任何关于 1.0V 电源轨的定义或转换路径**。因此，无法确认该以太网 PHY 是否使用 1.0V 电源，也无法说明其一级级供电方式。 **结论**：基于现有证据，无法回答以太网 PHY 的 1.0V 电源如何供出，因为既未指明 PHY 型号及其电源需求，系统电源树中也不存在 1.0V 电源轨。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F12</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，基于LN10046设计，给系统提供电源。 The KL30 to VCC3V3 is a first-level DC-DC based on the LN10046 design and provides power to the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用LN10046电源DCDC芯片，将12V系统电源转换为3.3V，提供给系统供电使用。 The LN10046 power supply DCDC chip is used to convert the 12V system power supply to 3.3V and provide power supply to the system for use. </td></tr></table> | HW Interface Component ：QM; 问题：U1700 的替代料和采购单价是多少？
参考答案：当前知识库只有电路和 HSI 文档，没有 BOM、替代料或价格表证据；只能确认 U1700 的已知料号为 20200424007，不能编造替代料或单价。
实际回答：根据现有证据，针对您的问题“U1700 的替代料和采购单价是多少？”回答如下： ### 子问题1：U1700 的替代料有哪些？ - **状态**：部分覆盖（partial） - **支持证据**：电路设计文件中记录 U1700 的实例信息为 **LP87702-Q1**，具体型号为 **LP87702RRHBRQ1**（来源：825504380_ADAS_SCH_TCN2.EDF，实例 U1700）。 - **缺口说明**：现有证据仅提供了 U1700 本身的型号，**未给出任何替代料（如兼容替换型号）**。缺失的证据类型为电子表格（spreadsheet_table）和文档文本（document_text），可能包含替代料清单但未检索到。 ### 子问题2：上述替代料的采购单价是多少？ - **状态**：缺失（missing） - **支持证据**：无。 - **缺口说明**：未找到任何关于 U1700 替代料采购单价的证据。缺失的证据类型为电子表格（spreadsheet_table），可能包含价格信息但未检索到。 ### 证据冲突说明 证据覆盖矩阵中列出了多个字段的冲突值（如“ecu connector”、“hw interface component”等），但这些冲突均与 U1700 直接无关，不影响本问题的结论。 ### 总结 - **U1700 替代料**：未找到，仅知 U1700 本身型号为 LP87702RRHBRQ1。 - **采购单价**：未找到任何信息。 如需进一步查询，建议补充检索电子表格或采购文档中与 U1700 替代料及价格相关的条目。

问题：主控 MCU 是哪颗，型号是什么？
参考答案：EDF 中 U900 为 SAK-TC367DP-64F300S，即 AURIX TC367；HSI 章节名称写作 TC377 最小系统。回答应披露 TC367 与 TC377 的来源差异，不能合并为无冲突的单一型号。
实际回答：根据提供的证据，主控MCU的型号已明确，具体信息如下： **SQ_1: 主控MCU的型号是什么？** - **型号: SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA** - **来源说明:** - 证据[10]（文档 600608964_ADAS_HSI_0506_1952_shoulin.wang.docx）的模块功能描述中明确写明：“32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有**SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**主控芯片”。 - 证据[14]（同一文档）的模块列表表格中，在“F01/MCU”模块的功能定义列同样列出了“**SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA**”。 - **缺失信息:** 无（主控MCU型号已完全覆盖）。 **证据冲突说明:** - 检索到的数据中，存在与MCU型号不直接相关的冲突（如电源电压、引脚网络名称等），但针对“主控MCU型号”这一字段，所有证据保持一致，**无冲突**。

system components: an external 30MHz crystal provides the basic clock input for EYEQ6-L, an external 20MHz active crystal provides the monitoring clock, an external 2GbNAND FLASH memory program for OSPI interface, and an external 1GB LPDDR4 operation memory. NXB0104 is used for level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8-SPI of NAND FLASH for MCU to read and write NAND FLASH.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td> 1、处理器 根据ADAS产品需求图像处理SOC功能安全需要达到ASIL-B，经综合评估选择Mobileye产品EYEQ6-L，最高功能安全可达到ASIL-B，接口资源丰富。 1.Processor According to the requirements of ADAS products, the functional safety of image processing SOC needs to reach ASIL-B. After comprehensive evaluation, Mobileye product EYEQ6-L is selected, and the highest functional safety can reach ASIL-B with abundant interface resources. + 2、时钟 硬件选型30MHz晶振给EYEQ6-L提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。外部20MHz有源晶振作为内部监控时钟。 2.Clock The 30MHz crystal provides clock input to the EYEQ6-L, and its internal PLL circuit completes frequency multiplication and frequency division for the EyeQ6-L core and each module. The external 20MHz active crystal is energized as an internal monitoring clock. 3、DDR EYEQ6在外扩的DDR中运行程序和存储临时数据，其DDR控制器可支持32bits位宽的LPDDR4 SDRAM，最大读写速率4266 Mb/s/pin 。本设计中选用1片32bit的LPDDR4 SDRAM，型号为车规级器件K4F8E3S4HD-GHCL02V，以充分利用数据带宽，保证EYEQ6的处理速度，LPDDR4工作环境温度为-40℃~105℃，单片总容量1GB。 3.DDR EYEQ6 runs programs and stores temporary data in the extended DDR. Its DDR controller supports LPDDR4 SDRAM with 32bits wide and a maximum read/write rate of 4266Mb/s/pin. In this design, a 32bit LPDDR4 SDRAM is selected, the model is car grade device K4F8E3S4HD-GHCLO2V, in order to make full use of the data bandwidth and ensure the processing speed of EYEQ6. The working environment temperature of LPDDR4 is -40℃-105℃, and the total capacity of a single chip is 1GB. 4、NAND FLASH 选择ME推荐的WinBond公司产品W35N02JWTBKF，容量2Gb，FLASH支持CRC安全机制。 NXB0104是用于MCU的3V3_ SPI和NAND FLASH的1V8_ SPI进行电平转换，通过MCU_SPI1_CS片选电平转换芯片，实现MCU对NAND FLASH的读写操作，转换芯片在本应用场景下支持最大传输速率80Mbps。 NAND FLASH Choose the WinBond company product W35N02JWTBKF recommended by ME, with a capacity of 2Gb and FLASH supporting CRC security mechanism. NXB0104 is used for the level conversion of 3V3_SPI of MCU and 1V8_SPI of NAND FLASH. The level conversion chip is selected by MCU_SPI1_CS chip to realize the reading and writing operation of MCU to NAND FLASH. The converter chip supports a maximum transmission rate of 80Mbps in this application scenario. 5、电源和复位 EYEQ6-L需要6路电源：VCC3V3_EQ、VCC0V75、0V75_MIPI_DDR、VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC1V8_EQ，其中VCC0V75用于给内核供电。复位信号由MCU通过P02.4口进行控制。 Power and reset EYEQ6-L requires six power sources: VCC3V3-EQ, VCCOV75, OV75_MIPI_DDR, VCC1V8, VCC1V1, VCC1V8_EQ, where VCCOV75 is used to power the core. The reset signal is controlled by the MCU through the P02.4 pin.</td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F01</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>32位MCU芯片TC377最小系统模块有SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA主控芯片和晶体、硬件配置组成，实现SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA最小硬件系统工作单元，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 32-bit MCU chip TC377 minimum system module has SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA main control chip and crystal, hardware configuration, to achieve SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA minimum hardware system work unit, functional safety level up to ASIL-D, meet the application requirements. </td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>处理器 根据ADAS产品需求，综合评估选型为32位MCU芯片SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA， MCU接口资源丰富，功能安全等级最高可达到ASIL-D，满足应用要求。 Processor According to ADAS product requirements, 32-bit MCU chip SAK-TC377TP-96F300S AA is selected through comprehensive evaluation, which has rich MCU interface resources and the highest functional safety level can reach ASIL-D to meet application requirements 时钟设计 硬件选型20MHz晶振给MCU提供时钟输入，由其内部PLL等电路完成倍频、分频，供其内核和各模块使用。 Clock design Hardware selection of clock design 20MHz crystal oscillator provides clock input to MCU, and its internal PLL and other circuits complete frequency doubling and frequency division for its kernel and each module. 复位及watchdog 系统复位及看门狗芯片采用LP87702内部看门狗，喂狗方式为窗口喂狗，当喂狗窗口无有效喂狗次数超过OTP配置时，将触发整个系统复位。 a) 上电时，看门狗芯片对MCU启动过程进行监控，同时对MCU的电源VCC3V3电压进行低压监控，异常时主动复位MCU。 b) 运行过程中由看门狗监控MCU运行状态，当MCU程序跑飞时看门狗超时触发复位信号主动复位MCU。 Reset and watchdog The system reset and watchdog chip adopts LP87702 internal watchdog, and the feeding method is window feeding. When the number of effective dog feeding in the feeding window exceeds the OTP configuration, the whole system reset will be triggered. When powering on, the watchdog chip monitors the start-up process of MCU, and at the same time, it monitors the low voltage voltage of MCU's power supply VCC3V3, and actively resets MCU when abnormal. MCU running status is monitored by watchdog during operation. When MCU program runs, watchdog timeout triggers reset signal and actively reset MCU. 电源需求 MCU需要1路VCC3V3_MCU（精度：3.3V±10%）和1路VCC1V25_MCU（精度：1.2-1.35V）供电。 4. 问题：U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络分别是什么？
参考答案：U1800 是 TPS62872，VIN 为 VCC3V3，VOSNS 为 VCC0V75，EN 接 N19888813，并通过连接到 MCU 的 SDA/SCL 进行 I²C 控制。
实际回答：好的，作为专业的硬件资料检索助手，我将严格基于您提供的证据，对您的问题进行回答。 ### 关于 U1800 的输入、输出检测、使能和 I²C 控制网络 根据现有证据，我可以提供以下信息。请注意，证据覆盖度评估为“部分覆盖”，这意味着部分信息存在，但部分关键信息缺失。 #### 1. U1800 的输入检测 * **基于证据的说明**：现有证据没有明确定义“输入检测”功能或相关的引脚。证据仅指出 U1800（型号 TPS62872）的输入电源是 **VCC3V3**。 * 来源 [2] (文档 chunk: 0eb6659a764f4d29) 和 [5] (文档 chunk: b6943ae371d76077) 均描述：“VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电”。 * 来源 [1] (电路图) 确认了 U1800 的型号为 TPS62872Y1QWRXSRQ1。 * **缺失信息**： * 缺乏 U1800 具体的输入电压检测引脚定义（如 PG 引脚）或输入检测功能的描述。 * 没有发现相关的表格或文档文本对此进行详细说明。 #### 2. U1800 的输出检测 * **基于证据的说明**：现有证据没有明确定义“输出检测”功能或相关的引脚。证据仅指出 U1800 的输出是 **VCC0V75**，用于 EQ6 的核供电。 * 来源 [1] (电路图) 和 [2] (文档) 均确认了其输出为 VCC0V75。 * **缺失信息**： * 缺乏 U1800 具体的输出电压检测引脚（如 PG 引脚）或输出检测功能的描述。 * 没有发现相关的表格或文档文本对此进行详细说明。 #### 3. U1800 的使能 * **基于证据的说明**：U1800 的使能引脚由 **MCU 的 P22.0 引脚** 控制。 * 来源 [2] (文档 chunk: 0eb6659a764f4d29) 明确说明：“用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片”。 * 来源 [5] (文档 chunk: b6943ae371d76077) 也确认了这一点：“P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip”。 * **缺失信息**：证据中没有明确指出使能电平（高电平有效或低电平有效），但根据常规逻辑推断，通常为高电平有效。此信息需要查阅芯片手册确认。 #### 4. U1800 的 I²C 控制网络 * **基于证据的说明**：U1800 的 I²C 控制网络连接到 MCU 的 **I²C 总线**，具体引脚为 **MCU_SDA_P15.5** (数据线) 和 **MCU_SCL_P15.4** (时钟线)。 * 来源 [2] (文档) 明确说明：“用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信”。 * 来源 [5] (文档) 也确认了这一点：“MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication”。 * **缺失信息**：证据中没有提供 U1800 的 I²C 设备地址。 ### 证据冲突说明 根据您提供的“证据覆盖度”信息，存在多个证据冲突。这些冲突主要涉及文档模板变更历史、一些无关参数（如“none”字段下的各种值）以及某些模块的组件归属等，但**没有直接证据表明这些冲突涉及 U1800 的输入、输出、使能或 I²C 控制网络**。因此，这些冲突不影响上述回答的结论。例如，冲突列表中的 `template change history` 和 `mcu component` 等字段的冲突，均未在 U1800 相关的直接证据中出现。 ### 总结与缺口声明 | 子问题 | 状态 | 结论与缺口 | | :--- | :--- | :--- | | **输入检测** | **部分覆盖** | **结论**：输入电源网络为 VCC3V3。<br>**缺口**：缺乏“输入检测”功能的具体定义、相关引脚或检测机制。 | | **输出检测** | **部分覆盖** | **结论**：输出电压网络为 VCC0V75。<br>**缺口**：缺乏“输出检测”功能的具体定义、相关引脚或检测机制。 | | **使能** | **部分覆盖** | **结论**：使能引脚连接到 MCU 的 P22.0 引脚。<br>**缺口**：使能电平（高/低有效）未在证据中明确说明。 | | **I²C 控制网络** | **部分覆盖** | **结论**：I²C 总线连接到 MCU 的 SDA (P15.5) 和 SCL (P15.4) 引脚。<br>**缺口**：U1800 的 I²C 设备地址未在证据中提供。 | **重要提示**：以上所有结论均严格基于您提供的证据。对于标记为“缺失”的信息，我无法编造，需要您查阅 U1800 (TPS62872) 的官方数据手册（Datasheet）来获取更完整的引脚功能定义和电气特性。

Function</td><td>系统使用车载电池12V电压供电，经过EMI电路后输入至电源转换芯片，转换输出系统各模块所需电源。 The system uses 12V voltage power supply from the vehicle battery, which is input to the power conversion chip through the EMI circuit. Then the power conversion chip convert and output the power required by each module of the system.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>KL30 EMI (ASIL-B) KL30供电口处增加EMI电路，用于供电保护和滤波。硬件电路设计在UBD网络增加两个串联ESD电容，PCB布局靠近主连接器引脚处，用于吸收外部ESD信号，后端增加双向TVS管用于瞬态过压保护 The EMI circuit is added at the KL30 power supply port for power supply protection and filtering. The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_ETH(QM) 3V3转3V3_ETH用于ETH供电，现选择负载开关方案。用MCU的P21.0引脚使能该芯片。 3V3 to 3V3_ETH for ETH power supply, the load switch scheme is selected. . The P21.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. The P20.0 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip.</td></tr><tr><td colspan='2'></td></tr></table> | <table><tr><td>HM ID</td><td>F15</td></tr><tr><td>模块功能 Module Function</td><td>VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR应用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed.</td></tr><tr><td>模块框图 Module diagram</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模块方案 Module solution</td><td>采用SA23002电源芯片，将VCC3V3转换为0V75_MIPI_DDR，提供给EQ6的IO口供电。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 SA23002 power chip is used to convert VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR to supply power to the I/O port of EQ6. UBSW_ACQ_AN6信号是UBD经过100K 1%、10K 1%电阻分压后的模拟信号。 UBSW_ACQ_AN6 signal is an analog signal of UBD divided by 100K 1%、10K 1% resistors. Which is connected to the ADC pin of MCU for sampling. 问题：VCC1V8 到 EQ 的 1.8V 电源经过哪颗负载开关，使能信号是什么？
参考答案：VCC1V8 经 U1803（TPS22918）输出为 VCC1V8_EQ，使能信号为 MCU_1V8_EQ_EN。
实际回答：根据检索到的证据，以下是对您问题的回答： ### 子问题1：VCC1V8到EQ的1.8V电源路径上经过哪些负载开关？ **回答：** 经过一颗型号为 **TPS22918** 的负载开关。 * **来源说明：** 检索到的文档[10]明确描述：“采用TPS22918负载开关，将VCC1V8电源转换为VCC1V8_EQ，给EQ6供电。” 文档[9]也提到：“VCC1V8转VCC1V8_EQ用于EQ供电，现选择负载开关方案。” 文档[15]中的模块列表将“F19”模块定义为“TPS22918 负载开关，VCC1V8电压输入，VCC1V8_EQ电压输出”。 ### 子问题2：这些负载开关的使能信号是什么？ **回答：** 使能信号是 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**，来自MCU的 **P20.0** 引脚。 * **来源说明：** 文档[9]和[10]均指出：“用MCU的P20.0引脚使能该芯片。” 文档[12]则更详细地列出了该信号，其名称为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**，功能定义为“EQ 1V8供电的使能信号”，并指明其连接到MCU的 **P20.0** 引脚。 ### 证据冲突说明 在检索的证据中，存在多处与当前问题无关的字段冲突，例如对同一模块的“HW Interface Component”字段（如“b”和“qm”）、ECU连接器引脚号等，这些冲突不影响对您问题的直接回答，但体现了数据源内部的不一致性。 ### 缺失信息 已检索到的证据完全覆盖了您的问题，没有缺失信息。 --- **总结：** VCC1V8到EQ的1.8V电源经过一颗型号为 **TPS22918** 的负载开关，其使能信号为 **MCU_1V8_EQ_EN**，由MCU的 **P20.0** 引脚控制。

The hardware circuit design adds two series ESD capacitors in the UBD network, the PCB layout is close to the main connector pin for absorbing external ESD signals, and the two-way TVS tube is added at the back end for transient overvoltage protection. KL30 to VCC3V3 (ASILB) KL30转VCC3V3是系统一级DC-DC，给MCU提供VCC3V3_MCU电源，所以系统要求其满足ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 KL30 to VCC3V3 is a system primary DC-DC chip, because the MCU needs VCC3V3_MCU power supply, so the system requires it to meet the ASILB to ensure the functional safety of the MCU. VCC3V3_MCU to VCC1V25_MCU(ASILB) 3V3转1V25_MCU是使用MCU内置控制器进行控制的BUCK电源，用于MCU的核供电，功能安全需求达到ASILB，确保MCU功能安全。 3V3 to 1V25_MCU is a BUCK power supply controlled by MCU built-in controller, which is used for MCU nuclear power supply. Functional safety requirements meet ASILB to ensure the functional safety of MCU. VCC3V3 to VCC0V75(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC0V75应用于EQ6的内核供电，要求输出电流能力大，现选型TI的电源方案TPS62872。用MCU的P22.0引脚使能芯片，用MCU_SDA_P15.5，MCU_SCL_P15.4引脚I2C通信来设置输出电压和通信。 VCC3V3 to VCC0V75 is applied to the core power supply of EQ6, which requires large output current capacity. The power supply scheme TPS62872 of TI is selected. P22.0 pin of MCU is used to enable the chip, and MCU_SDA_P15.5, MCU_SCL_P15.4 pin I2C communication is used to set the output voltage and communication. VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR(QM(B)) VCC3V3转0V75_MIPI_DDR用于EQ6的IO口供电，现使用矽力杰的SA23002进行设计。用MCU的P22.1引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to 0V75_MIPI_DDR for EQ6 IO port power supply, Silergy’s SA23002 is designed. The P22.1 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. VCC3V3转VCC1V8、VCC1V1、VCC50(QM(B)) VCC1V8用于EQ6、CAM、DDR、FLASH供电，VCC1V1用于EQ6、DDR供电，VCC50用于CAN收发器供电，现选择LP87702 PMIC方案。用MCU的P15.3引脚使能该芯片的5V输出，用MCU_SCL_P15.4，MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C通信来设置1.8V和1.1V输出电压和使能。 VCC1V8 is used for EQ6, CAM, DDR, FLASH power supply, VCC1V1 is used for EQ6, DDR power supply, VCC50 is used for CAN transceiver power supply, LP87702 PMIC scheme is selected. P15.3 pin of MCU is used to enable 5V output of the chip, and MCU_SCL_P15.4, MCU_SDA_P15.5 I2C communication is used to set 1.8V and 1.1V output voltage and enable it. VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ(QM(B)) VCC3V3转VCC3V3_EQ用于EQ6的IO和摄像头供电，现使用负载开关进行设计。用MCU的P20.3引脚使能该芯片。 VCC3V3 to VCC3V3_EQ for EQ6’s IO and camera power supply, now using the load switch is designed. .The P20.3 pin of the MCU is used to enable the chip. After EQ6 runs, RESET_N is configured to high level, and CAM parameters are configured through I2C, and the camera starts to work. Run mode The camera operates normally and outputs the image information to EQ6. Reset mode EQ6 config the RESET_N signal of the camera to be low and the CAM enters the reset mode. Power off mode MCU controls the power down of EQ6 system (VCC3V3_EQ, VCC1V8).</td></tr><tr><td>模式切换接口 Mode switching interface</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式状态机 Mode state machine</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>模式切换条件 Mode switching condition</td><td>参考： 产品系统需求规格说明 产品系统方案设计说明书 Reference: Product system requirement specification Product system scheme design specification.</td></tr><tr><td>备注 Note</td><td>摄像头上需要3路电源（VCC3V3、VCC1V8、VCC1V1），其中VCC1V8和VCC3V3是主板提供，VCC1V1由3V3转换得到。 摄像头复位信号由EQ6通过GPIO口进行控制。 摄像头3路电源的上下电和复位信号的时序要求如下所示： The camera needs 3 power sources (VCC2V8, VCC1V8, VCC1V05), VCC1V8 and VCC3V3 are provided by the Main board, VCC1V1 is converted from 3V3. 问题：文档声明板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块，回答时能否用网表确认其具体连接？
参考答案：HSI 硬件架构包含 CAN/CAN FD 电路；具体器件和网络连接应以 EDF 检索结果为准，文档能力声明与网表拓扑需分别标注来源。
实际回答：好的，根据您提供的证据，我将严格依据文档和网表信息，组织回答如下。 ### 板上有哪些 CAN 相关模块？ **回答：** 板上共有 **4 路 CAN 通讯模块**，分别标识为 **CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3**。 * **来源说明：** * 文档[19]（硬件模块列表）明确指出模块 `F23` (CAN0), `F22` (CAN1), `F21` (CAN2), `F20` (CAN3) 均为CAN通讯模块。 * 文档[18]（模块方案）也说明“ADAS系统需求MCU外置4路CAN/CAN FD和外部进行通讯”，并详细列出了CAN0、CAN1、CAN2、CAN3的引脚分配。 * **模块详情：** * **CAN0 (F23):** 基于 TCAN1044A 的 CAN 通讯 [19]。 * **CAN1 (F22):** 基于 TCAN1044A 的 CAN 通讯 [19]。 * **CAN2 (F21):** 基于 TCAN1044A 的 CAN 通讯 [19]。 * **CAN3 (F20):** 基于 TCAN1145 的 CAN 通讯，硬件支持特定报文唤醒 [19]。 * **注意：** 文档[18]指出，所有四路CAN/CAN FD均兼容TCAN1145和TCAN1044A，根据客户需求进行选贴。 ### 这些 CAN 模块的具体连接如何？ **回答：** 可以，根据网表（原理图信息）可以确认各 CAN 模块的具体连接。以下是依据网表[1]-[16]梳理出的关键网络连接。 **1. CAN0 模块连接：** * **差分信号总线：** * `CAN0H` 网络连接到：C1201, D1200, L1200, R1209, R1214, TP1206, X1900 [1]。 * `CAN0L` 网络连接到：C1200, D1200, L1200, R1212, R1215, TP1215, X1900 [2]。 * **控制与状态信号：** * `CAN0_INH` (唤醒控制) 网络连接到：D1203, R1630, TP1219, U1200 (INH), U1203 (INH) [3]。 * `CAN0_INH_ACQ` (唤醒状态采集) 网络连接到：C1216, R1236, R1237, TP1232, U900A (MCU的AN20引脚) [4]。 * `CAN0_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS5_P02.8` (待机/SPI片选) 网络连接到：R1126, R1228, R1241, TP1217, U1200 (SCSN), U1202 (STB), U1203 (NSTB) [5]。 **2. CAN1 模块连接：** * **差分信号总线：** * `CAN1H` 网络连接到：C1207, D1201, L1201, R1220, R1224, TP1222, X1900 [6]。 * `CAN1L` 网络连接到：C1206, D1201, L1201, R1223, R1225, TP1223, X1900 [7]。 * **控制与状态信号：** * `CAN1_INH` 网络连接到：D1204, R1629, TP1228, U1204 (INH), U1205 (INH) [8]。 * 未提供 `CAN1_INH_ACQ` 和 `CAN1_STB` 的网表信息，但根据文档[18]，其STB信号为 `CAN1_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS4_P00.2`。 **3. CAN2 模块连接：** * **差分信号总线：** * `CAN2H` 网络连接到：C1301, D1300, L1300, R1302, R1306, TP1301, X1900 [9]。 * `CAN2L` 网络连接到：C1300, D1300, L1300, R1305, R1307, TP1303, X1900 [10]。 * **控制与状态信号：** * `CAN2_INH` 网络连接到：D1303, R1628, TP1314, U1302 (INH), U1303 (INH) [11]。 * `CAN2_INH_ACQ` 网络连接到：C1314, R1329, R1330, TP1321, U900A (MCU的AN22引脚) [12]。 * 未提供 `CAN2_STB` 的网表信息，但根据文档[18]，其STB信号为 `CAN2_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS3_P00.5`。 **4. CAN3 模块连接：** * **差分信号总线：** * `CAN3H` 网络连接到：C1306, D1301, L1301, R1311, R1315, TP1307, X1900 [13]。 * `CAN3L` 网络连接到：C1305, D1301, L1301, R1314, R1316, TP1309, X1900 [14]。 * **控制与状态信号：** * `CAN3_INH` 网络连接到：D1304, R1609, TP1319, U1304 (INH), U1305 (INH) [15]。 * `CAN3_INH_ACQ` 网络连接到：C1315, R1331, R1332, TP1322, U900A (MCU的AN23引脚) [16]。 * 未提供 `CAN3_STB` 的网表信息，但根据文档[18]，其STB信号为 `CAN3_STB_MCU_SPI3_CS6_P00.8`。 **共同连接点：** * 所有四路CAN的差分信号对（H/L）均连接到同一个连接器 `X1900` 的不同引脚，用于对外通信。 * CAN0和CAN2的INH信号（唤醒控制）还连接到了MCU的ADC引脚进行状态采集，用于唤醒逻辑判断 [4][12][20]。 * CAN0的STB信号同时也连接到了U1202和U1203，表明它可能控制多个收发器 [5]。 **缺失信息：** * 本证据中未提供 `CAN1_STB`, `CAN2_STB`, `CAN3_STB` 和 `CAN1_INH_ACQ` 的网表连接细节。虽然文档[18]提到了这些信号的MCU引脚分配，但未提供原理图级别的具体连接路径。

The overall hardware architecture is described as follows:整体系统由摄像头板（CAM）和主板（MB）两部分组成。The overall system is composed of two parts: camera board (CAM) and mainboard (MB).前向摄像头采集道路图像后通过数字接口输出视频数据。The forward camera collects road images and outputs video data through the digital interface.数字视频数据通过线缆传输到主板上的视频处理器EYEQ6进行视频处理。The digital video data is transmitted to the video processor EYEQ6 on the mainboard through cables for video processing.EYEQ6处理视频数据，输出路况信息。EYEQ6 processes video data and outputs road condition information.路况信息通过SPI口传送给MCU进行处理，主板通过CAN对外传送路况信息。Road condition information is transmitted to MCU through SPI interface for processing, and the mainboard transmits road condition information externally through CAN.处理结果通过CAN发出，包括对车的主动安全控制信号以及给驾驶员的报警信号。The processing result is sent out through CAN, including the active safety control signal for the car and the alarm signal for the driver.图 1 系统工作方式Fig 1 System operating block硬件框图 Hardware block diagram图 2 系统框图Fig 2 system
